      "text": "{\n  \"name\": \"acceptance-before\",\n  \"command\": [\n    \"bun\",\n    \"--no-install\",\n    \"test\",\n    \"./tenant.acceptance.test.mjs\"\n  ],\n  \"cwd\": \"/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working\",\n  \"exitCode\": 1,\n  \"stdout\": \"bun test v1.4.2 (50a8a8387)\\n\",\n  \"stderr\": \"\\ntenant.acceptance.test.mjs:\\n2 |   #items = new Map();\\n3 |   put(sku, quantity) { if (!sku || !Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) throw new Error('invalid item'); this.#items.set(sku, quantity); }\\n4 |   get(sku) { return this.#items.get(sku) ?? 0; }\\n5 |   remove(sku) { this.#items.delete(sku); }\\n6 | }\\n7 | export class TenantInventory { constructor() { throw new Error('tenant ownership not implemented'); } }\\n                                                                                                     ^\\nerror: tenant ownership not implemented\\n      at new TenantInventory (/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/inventory.mjs:7:98)\\n      at <anonymous> (/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-workflow-013-20261016/input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/tenant.acceptance.test.mjs:1:175)\\n(fail) tenant-scoped ownership across shared backend [0.25ms]\\n\\n 0 pass\\n 1 fail\\nRan 1 test across 1 file. [3.00ms]\\n\"\n}"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/example.mjs",
      "bytes": 355,
      "sha256": "ad8b1f47f594ee27276ccefad953e75add2182a494974ed353722b9d8a1ba823",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { TenantInventory } from './inventory.mjs';\n\nconst backend = new Map();\nconst company = new TenantInventory('company', backend);\nconst supplier = new TenantInventory('supplier', backend);\ncompany.put('sprocket', 7);\nsupplier.put('sprocket', 2);\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({ company: company.get('sprocket'), supplier: supplier.get('sprocket') }));\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/README.md",
      "bytes": 237,
      "sha256": "0f788464b62966cab555b5cf2ef73ad70af5cae94e71b24aaccae4719c0f6a86",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Single tenant inventory\nOne in-memory Inventory stores SKU quantities for a single company. Item identity is currently only SKU; callers share one store. API: put/get/remove. Types and example are part of the affected public boundary.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/types.ts",
      "bytes": 328,
      "sha256": "672b4e63b9e3ca157e49989335b668a81ccb73fac0cb34915eadabe7c389cab6",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export type InventoryBackend = Map<string, Map<string, number>>;\n\nexport interface Item {\n  readonly tenantId: string;\n  sku: string;\n  quantity: number;\n}\n\nexport interface TenantInventoryPort {\n  readonly tenantId: string;\n  put(sku: string, quantity: number): void;\n  get(sku: string): number;\n  remove(sku: string): void;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/tenant.acceptance.test.mjs",
      "bytes": 649,
      "sha256": "15a5e3d1f1d1799b930cf9aea2ff44c7d906542188d8c35dca5cb22f704635a3",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';test('tenant-scoped ownership across shared backend',()=>{const shared=new Map();const a=new TenantInventory('tenant-a',shared),b=new TenantInventory('tenant-b',shared);a.put('same-sku',7);b.put('same-sku',2);expect(a.get('same-sku')).toBe(7);expect(b.get('same-sku')).toBe(2);a.remove('same-sku');expect(a.get('same-sku')).toBe(0);expect(b.get('same-sku')).toBe(2);a.put('tenant-b:sku',3);b.put('sku',8);expect(a.get('tenant-b:sku')).toBe(3);expect(b.get('sku')).toBe(8);expect(()=>new TenantInventory('',shared)).toThrow();expect(()=>a.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/baseline.test.mjs",
      "bytes": 402,
      "sha256": "64ccfa767049c12b8916ba83746353f44d0fb418e4f23f173fc5a98c824c9892",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { test, expect } from 'bun:test';\nimport { TenantInventory } from './inventory.mjs';\n\ntest('original quantity behavior with an explicit owner', () => {\n  const inventory = new TenantInventory('company', new Map());\n  inventory.put('s', 3);\n  expect(inventory.get('s')).toBe(3);\n  inventory.remove('s');\n  expect(inventory.get('s')).toBe(0);\n  expect(() => inventory.put('s', -1)).toThrow();\n});\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/inventory.mjs",
      "bytes": 1474,
      "sha256": "064519ab1b6e9fdd2545c6e05ee9ae2993da222d13454255d442228a5654f2ae",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "function requireIdentity(value, name) {\n  if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.trim().length === 0) {\n    throw new TypeError(`${name} must be a nonblank string`);\n  }\n}\n\nexport class TenantInventory {\n  #tenantId;\n  #items;\n\n  constructor(tenantId, sharedBackendMap) {\n    requireIdentity(tenantId, 'tenantId');\n    if (!(sharedBackendMap instanceof Map)) {\n      throw new TypeError('sharedBackendMap must be a Map');\n    }\n    if (sharedBackendMap.has(tenantId)) {\n      const items = sharedBackendMap.get(tenantId);\n      if (!(items instanceof Map)) {\n        throw new TypeError('tenant bucket must be a Map');\n      }\n      for (const [sku, quantity] of items) {\n        requireIdentity(sku, 'sku');\n        if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) {\n          throw new TypeError('quantity must be a nonnegative integer');\n        }\n      }\n      this.#items = items;\n    } else {\n      this.#items = new Map();\n      sharedBackendMap.set(tenantId, this.#items);\n    }\n    this.#tenantId = tenantId;\n  }\n\n  get tenantId() {\n    return this.#tenantId;\n  }\n\n  put(sku, quantity) {\n    requireIdentity(sku, 'sku');\n    if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) {\n      throw new TypeError('quantity must be a nonnegative integer');\n    }\n    this.#items.set(sku, quantity);\n  }\n\n  get(sku) {\n    requireIdentity(sku, 'sku');\n    return this.#items.get(sku) ?? 0;\n  }\n\n  remove(sku) {\n    requireIdentity(sku, 'sku');\n    this.#items.delete(sku);\n  }\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/working/inventory.d.mts",
      "bytes": 351,
      "sha256": "59f5c9316acd72cf7aae8453245b2af68bb1d30800cce529c9de7105e8c99a0e",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import type { InventoryBackend, TenantInventoryPort } from './types';\n\nexport declare class TenantInventory implements TenantInventoryPort {\n  #private;\n  constructor(tenantId: string, sharedBackendMap: InventoryBackend);\n  get tenantId(): string;\n  put(sku: string, quantity: number): void;\n  get(sku: string): number;\n  remove(sku: string): void;\n}\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/check.mjs",
      "bytes": 1650,
      "sha256": "253b281359e0557e8960ec0e8295a6b28f15d1578d3d611dac974969621e36b0",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import { writeFileSync, readFileSync, appendFileSync } from 'node:fs';\nimport { resolve } from 'node:path';\nimport { createHash } from 'node:crypto';\n\nconst phase = process.argv[2];\nif (!['increment-1', 'increment-2'].includes(phase)) throw new Error('Unknown check phase');\nconst digest = path => createHash('sha256').update(readFileSync(path)).digest('hex');\nconst commands = phase === 'increment-1'\n  ? [['test', './baseline.test.mjs', './tenant.acceptance.test.mjs'], ['./example.mjs']]\n  : [['test'], ['./example.mjs']];\nconst records = [];\nfor (const args of commands) {\n  const command = ['bun', '--no-install', ...args];\n  const result = Bun.spawnSync(command, { cwd: resolve('outputs/working'), stdout: 'pipe', stderr: 'pipe', env: { ...process.env, BUN_INSTALL_AUTO: 'disable' } });\n  const record = { command, cwd: resolve('outputs/working'), exitCode: result.exitCode, stdout: result.stdout.toString(), stderr: result.stderr.toString() };\n  records.push(record);\n  console.log(JSON.stringify(record));\n}\nconst acceptance = { original: digest('input/tenant.acceptance.test.mjs'), working: digest('outputs/working/tenant.acceptance.test.mjs') };\nconst receipt = { phase, at: new Date().toISOString(), records, acceptance, compiler: { executed: false, reason: 'No compiler supplied in inert inputs; Bun tests execute JavaScript and do not typecheck declarations.' } };\nwriteFileSync(`outputs/evidence/${phase}.json`, JSON.stringify(receipt, null, 2));\nappendFileSync('outputs/evidence/increments.jsonl', JSON.stringify(receipt) + '\\n');\nif (records.some(r => r.exitCode !== 0) || acceptance.original !== acceptance.working) process.exit(1);\n"
    },

      "sha256": "49b0d574910574c87ed5b77ed0c51badeccc2962d555b40239c9c31a4f72831e",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Execution checklist\n\n- [ ] Verify task input SHA and frozen plugin identity.\n- [ ] Read complete task contract, request, all owned inputs, and required frozen source.\n- [ ] Discover exposed host tools and worker availability; record model observations.\n- [ ] Copy inert inputs to outputs/working.\n- [ ] Observe green baseline and red new-tenant acceptance before implementation.\n- [ ] Compare fresh tenant-ownership design with current design and implement coherently.\n- [ ] Propagate types, documentation, examples, rationale, and tests; preserve acceptance bytes.\n- [ ] Run actual checks and inspect artifacts.\n- [ ] Write result.md and completion.json with evidence and unresolved predicates.\n\nConstraints: only new-requirement; frozen plugin 0.1.3 at 51f209daf83cd97d64f89881d66533bb59dc57db; task input SHA 5146b42e798a406216aba005ce1f56470d6ce77c. Writes/execution artifacts only ./outputs. No package edits, installation, external writes, commits, deletion, settings, or accounts. Twenty-minute hard budget.\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/design.md",
      "bytes": 4318,
      "sha256": "82b6fbe4b41119cb6d8b79428421ad192e60cf9a73662f2d0a58539e3411fa16",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Tenant ownership design\n\nThe existing Inventory allocates one private Map keyed by SKU. Its constructor accepts no owner or backend. put validates a truthy SKU and a nonnegative integer. get returns zero for an absent SKU. remove deletes it. TenantInventory is a throwing stub. The baseline, public port, README and example all describe the unscoped API. All six inert input files were read before this design.\n\nA fresh design starts with an inventory belonging to exactly one tenant. The shared backend groups quantities by tenant, then SKU. The public constructor requires a tenant and a backend. An instance holds its validated immutable owner and the owner's bucket. Two instances for the same tenant share that bucket. Instances for different tenants allocate different buckets. No operation constructs a combined identity string.\n\n| Design | Ownership | Cost and failure mode |\n| --- | --- | --- |\n| Current private SKU Map | No tenant owner, no shared backend | Cannot satisfy shared storage or tenant ownership. |\n| Nested Maps, selected | Outer Map owns tenant buckets, inner Map owns SKUs | Direct lookup and ordinary string equality. Raw backend access remains trusted. |\n| Flat Map of record identities | Each record carries tenant and SKU | A fresh tuple is a different Map key. Correct lookup requires scanning or a separate identity index. |\n\nConcatenated keys are excluded by the request. Keeping an unscoped compatibility class would preserve an ownerless path. Remove it and migrate every owned caller in the same implementation increment. Existing quantities need an explicitly chosen tenant and an inner Map. No implicit default tenant or automatic migration runs.\n\nThe intended interface is `new TenantInventory(tenantId, backend)`, with `put(sku, quantity)`, `get(sku)` and `remove(sku)`. Tenant identity and SKU must be nonblank strings. Validation uses trim only to detect blank strings and preserves valid identity bytes. Quantity must be a nonnegative integer. Missing quantities return zero. Zero remains a stored quantity. The owner is read-only through a getter. The constructor validates the backend Map and any existing selected bucket. Direct backend mutation is a trusted caller responsibility.\n\nRedesign from First Principles replaced the ownerless structure. Model the Domain selected nested Maps. Boundary Discipline placed validation in the constructor and public methods. Type System Discipline keeps ownership required in the port, constructor declaration and Item records. Sequence Work into Verifiable Units requires a runtime check before the next increment. Prove It Works requires running the immutable acceptance against the actual copied module.\n\nThe actual contract allows direct execution. Required mode and agent files were read as requested context. This run activates only new-requirement, not the mode's separate Feature, Architect, Arena or PR workflows. The alternatives above are coordinator design reasoning. They are not independent candidates or review. Parent model is unobserved. Worker request policy would be inherit-parent; actual workers are none.\n\n## Throughput checkpoint\n\n- [x] Blocking first steps. SHA verified, all inputs read, baseline observed green and acceptance observed red before implementation.\n- [x] Independent workstreams. n/a. Runtime, declarations and caller migration form one small coupled change.\n- [x] Shared mutable state. One coordinator owns outputs/working. No worker writes. Runtime tenants own separate inner Maps inside the required shared backend.\n- [x] Smallest safe decomposition. One owner implements the domain and caller migration, then runs tests. Documentation and extra behavior checks follow as a second verified increment.\n\n## Delivery increments\n\n1. Replace the runtime, add its module declaration, update public types, migrate baseline and example, and check the baseline plus immutable acceptance and example.\n2. Add boundary and collision regressions, document rationale and migration, run all tests and example, inspect the diff and compare acceptance bytes.\n\nNo commits, PR, external operations or package changes are authorized. The existing frozen plugin Git commit is operator-specified. Version 0.1.3 and hashes of all read source bytes are observed; independent package Git provenance is unverified.\n"
    },

frozen = '/Users/digital-hawk/Herd/pstack-host-verification-20261016-51f209d/runtime/plugins/cache/pstack-frozen-verification/pstack-openai/0.1.3';\nconst hash = bytes => createHash('sha256').update(bytes).digest('hex');\nconst sources = [\n  'references/agent-prompts/poteto-agent.md', 'skills/poteto-mode/SKILL.md',\n  'docs/principles/README.md', 'skills/principle-redesign-from-first-principles/SKILL.md',\n  'references/host-capabilities.md', 'references/orchestration-contract.md',\n  'references/model-roles.md', 'docs/parity/cases/skills/principle-redesign-from-first-principles.json', 'plugin.json',\n];\nmkdirSync('outputs/evidence/source', { recursive: true });\nmkdirSync('outputs/working', { recursive: true writeFileSync(artifact, bytes);\n  manifest.push({ path: `${frozen}/${file}`, sha256: hash(bytes), bytes: bytes.length, artifact });\n}\nfor (const file of ['task-contract.json', 'request.md', ...readdirSync('input').map(f => `input/${f}`)]) {\n  const bytes = readFileSync(file);\n  const artifact = `outputs/evidence/source/${file.replaceAll('/', '__')}`;\n  writeFileSync(artifact, bytes);\n  manifest.push({ path: resolve(file), sha256: hash(bytes), bytes: bytes.length, artifact });\n  if (file.startsWith('input/')) copyFileSync(file, `outputs/working/${file.slice(6)}`);\n}\nwriteFileSync('outputs/evidence/read-manifest.json', JSON.stringify(manifest, null, 2));\nconst revision = Bun.spawnSync(['git', 'rev-parse', 'HEAD'], { stdout: 'pipe', stderr: 'pipe' });\nconst status = Bun.spawnSync(['git', 'status', '--short'], { stdout: 'pipe', stderr: 'pipe' });\nconst identity = { inputRevision: revision.stdout.toString().trim(), expectedInputRevision: '5146b42e798a406216aba005ce1f56470d6ce77c', revisionExit: revision.exitCode, revisionStderr: revision.stderr.toString(), status: status.stdout.toString(), version: JSON.parse(readFileSync(`${frozen}/plugin.json`, 'utf8')).version, sourceFreeze: '51f209daf83cd97d64f89881d66533bb59dc57db', freezeVerification: 'Operator-specified frozen path and version observed; file hashes retained. No independent package Git-commit attestation.' };\nwriteFileSync('outputs/evidence/identity.json', JSON.stringify(identity, null, 2));\nif (identity.inputRevision !== identity.expectedInputRevision || identity.version !== '0.1.3') throw new Error('Identity mismatch');\nfor (const [name, args] of [['baseline-before', ['test', './baseline.test.mjs']], ['acceptance-before', ['test', './tenant.acceptance.test.mjs']]]) {\n  const result = Bun.spawnSync(['bun', '--no-install', ...args], { cwd: resolve('outputs/working'), stdout: 'pipe', stderr: 'pipe', env: { ...process.env, BUN_INSTALL_AUTO: 'disable' } });\n  const record = { name, command: ['bun', '--no-install', ...args], cwd: resolve('outputs/working'), exitCode: result.exitCode, stdout: result.stdout.toString(), stderr: result.stderr.toString() };\n  writeFileSync(`outputs/evidence/${name}.json`, JSON.stringify(record, null, 2));\n  console.log(JSON.stringify(record));\n  if (name === 'baseline-before' && result.exitCode !== 0) throw new Error('Baseline not green');\n  if (name === 'acceptance-before' && result.exitCode === 0) throw new Error('Acceptance not red');\n}\n"

      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/input/types.ts",

      "sha256": "136b8c17ee0a48a57a1a156e0450afe36ae9158b19175547054525edd12b5026",

    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/input/tenant.acceptance.test.mjs",
      "bytes": 649,
      "sha256": "15a5e3d1f1d1799b930cf9aea2ff44c7d906542188d8c35dca5cb22f704635a3",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';test('tenant-scoped ownership across shared backend',()=>{const shared=new Map();const a=new TenantInventory('tenant-a',shared),b=new TenantInventory('tenant-b',shared);a.put('same-sku',7);b.put('same-sku',2);expect(a.get('same-sku')).toBe(7);expect(b.get('same-sku')).toBe(2);a.remove('same-sku');expect(a.get('same-sku')).toBe(0);expect(b.get('same-sku')).toBe(2);a.put('tenant-b:sku',3);b.put('sku',8);expect(a.get('tenant-b:sku')).toBe(3);expect(b.get('sku')).toBe(8);expect(()=>new TenantInventory('',shared)).toThrow();expect(()=>a.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/input/baseline.test.mjs",
      "bytes": 260,
      "sha256": "780e21aa976a0bf4bdf39663976812dc380d8f0188536911ddb345db94e236e5",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import {test,expect} from 'bun:test'; import {Inventory} from './inventory.mjs'; test('single tenant baseline',()=>{const i=new Inventory();i.put('s',3);expect(i.get('s')).toBe(3);i.remove('s');expect(i.get('s')).toBe(0);expect(()=>i.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\n"
    },
    {
      "path": "input-gap-repairs/new-requirement/input/inventory.mjs",
      "bytes": 392,
      "sha256": "4724a13747ddd04ad374309bf3c9313b8fed867cd1afbf1fa72bf9eb27ff81c5",
      "owner": "local-013-input-gap-repair-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "export class Inventory {\n  #items = new Map();\n  put(sku, quantity) { if (!sku || !Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) throw new Error('invalid item'); this.#items.set(sku, quantity); }\n  get(sku) { return this.#items.get(sku) ?? 0; }\n  remove(sku) { this.#items.delete(sku); }\n}\nexport class TenantInventory { constructor() { throw new Error('tenant ownership not implemented'); } }\n"
    },

      "text": "# Direct local tenant repair\n\nSource 0.1.3, freeze 51f209daf83cd97d64f89881d66533bb59dc57db. Input 5146b42e798a406216aba005ce1f56470d6ce77c. This is a separate native root-agent attempt; the earlier incomplete CLI attempt is preserved.\n\nRead all six affected committed files. Compared a tenant-rooted design with a method-parameter retrofit and concatenated keys. Chose required immutable ownership and nested tenant/SKU Maps. Increment1 replaced the domain root and migrated the fixture baseline; both baseline and unchanged tenant acceptance were then green. Increment2 propagated API types/declarations, docs, runnable example and adjacent checks.\n\nObserved original baseline GREEN, unchanged tenant acceptance RED, then two-check increment GREEN and seven-test final suite GREEN. Existing strict compiler exited0 on API declarations/consumer plus expected type-error checks; this does not statically check the JavaScript implementation. Runnable example emitted company7/supplier2 from one shared backend. Immutable acceptance bytes and tracked original input remained unchanged. No commits, installs, external writes or production migration.\n\nArtifacts: assignment.json, checklist.md, execute.mjs, outputs/affected-source-read.json, design.md, working/*, checks.json and summary.json. Actual root model gpt-6.1-sol medium is observed in trail-review-013/model-observation.json. No independent workers required by this principle; no model diversity claimed. No grade assigned by producer.\n"

Redesign as if the requirement had been a foundational assumption from day one, instead of bolting it on.\\\"\\ndisable-model-invocation: true\\n---\\n\\n# Redesign From First Principles\\n\\nWhen integrating a change, don't bolt it onto the existing design. Redesign as if the requirement had been there from the start.\\n\\n- Read all affected files and understand the current design\\n- Ask: \\\"if we were writing this from scratch with this new requirement, what would we build?\\\"\\n- Propagate the change through every reference: types, docs, examples, rationale sections\\n- Think about the whole redesign, then deliver it incrementally\\n\\nThis is the method for preserving option value when integrating changes into an existing design.\\n\\n## Capability and permission boundaries\\n\\nUse the [session capability contract](../../references/host-capabilities.md) before reading source, editing files or running checks. Apply the redesign within the authorized task. Propagating references does not grant permission to change unrelated files or perform external actions.\\n\\nIf source is available but writes or execution are unavailable, provide a concrete redesign and incremental sequence as reduced capability. State which edits and checks were not performed. If affected source or design context is missing, identify the missing inputs and keep any redesign provisional. 'bun:test';import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';test('tenant-scoped ownership across shared backend',()=>{const shared=new Map();const a=new TenantInventory('tenant-a',shared),b=new direct-repairs/new-requirement/execute.mjs <<'EOF'\\nimport{mkdir,readFile,cp,writeFile}from'node:fs/promises';import{createHash}from'node:crypto';\\nconst root=import.meta.dir,out=root+'/outputs',working=out+'/working',records=[];await mkdir(out,{recursive:true});\\nconst hash=b=>createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');\\nlet reads=[];for(const function run(label,argv,cwd){let start=new Date().toISOString(),p=Bun.spawn(argv,{cwd,stdout:'pipe',stderr:'pipe'}),[stdout,stderr,exitCode]=await Promise.all([new Response(p.stdout).text(),new Response(p.stderr).text(),p.exited]);let r={label,argv,cwd,startedAt:start,finishedAt:new Date().toISOString(),exitCode,stdout,stderr};records.push(r);await writeFile(out+'/checks.json',JSON.stringify(records,null,2)+'\\\\n');return r}\\nlet old=await run('original single-tenant baseline GREEN',['bun','test','baseline.test.mjs'],root+'/input/input');if(old.exitCode!==0)throw Error('baseline');let red=await run('original immutable tenant acceptance RED',['bun','test','tenant.acceptance.test.mjs'],root+'/input/input');if(red.exitCode===0)throw Error('acceptance unexpectedly green');\\nawait writeFile(out+'/design.md',`# Tenant-first design before implementation\\\\n\\\\nThe original Inventory gives each object one private SKU Map and has no ownership boundary. Adding a tenant parameter to put/get would preserve the wrong root and repeat ownership at every call. Concatenating tenant and SKU introduces delimiter collisions.\\\\n\\\\nThe domain root is instead TenantInventory with a required, immutable tenantId. Shared storage is Map<tenantId, Map<sku,quantity>>; identity is structurally separated before values are serialized. Each constructor validates owner and backend. Each method validates its public input. Two instances of the same owner share one bucket; different owners cannot observe/remove each other's SKU.\\\\n\\\\nIncrement 1 replaces the root and migrates the fixture baseline, then runs baseline+unchanged acceptance. Increment 2 propagates ownership through API declarations, type consumer, docs, runnable example and adjacent checks, then executes all checks. Inventory export is deliberately removed; the migration is explicit rather than retained as a throwaway wrapper. All edits stay in outputs/working and pristine committed inputs remain outside.\\\\n`);\\nawait cp(root+'/input/input',working,{recursive:true});\\nawait writeFile(working+'/inventory.mjs',`const identifier = (value, label) => { if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.trim() === '') throw new Error('invalid ' + label); return value; };\\\\nexport class TenantInventory {\\\\n  #tenantId; #backend;\\\\n  constructor(tenantId, sharedBackend = new Map()) {\\\\n    this.#tenantId = identifier(tenantId, 'tenant');\\\\n    if (!(sharedBackend instanceof Map)) throw new Error('invalid backend');\\\\n    const bucket = sharedBackend.get(tenantId);\\\\n    if (bucket !== undefined && !(bucket instanceof Map)) throw new Error('invalid tenant storage');\\\\n    if (bucket === undefined) sharedBackend.set(tenantId, new Map());\\\\n    this.#backend = sharedBackend;\\\\n    Object.defineProperty(this, 'tenantId', {value: tenantId, writable: false, configurable: false, enumerable: true});\\\\n  }\\\\n  #items() { const items = this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId); if (!(items instanceof Map)) throw new Error('invalid tenant storage'); return items; }\\\\n  put(sku, quantity) { identifier(sku, 'sku'); if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) throw new Error('invalid quantity'); this.#items().set(sku, quantity); }\\\\n  get(sku) { identifier(sku, 'sku'); return this.#items().get(sku) ?? 0; }\\\\n  remove(sku) { identifier(sku, 'sku'); this.#items().delete(sku); }\\\\n}\\\\n`);\\nawait writeFile(working+'/baseline.test.mjs',`import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';test('migrated single-owner baseline',()=>{const i=new TenantInventory('baseline');i.put('s',3);expect(i.get('s')).toBe(3);i.remove('s');expect(i.get('s')).toBe(0);expect(()=>i.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\\\\n`);\\nlet green=await run('increment1 baseline and unchanged tenant acceptance GREEN',['bun','test','baseline.test.mjs','tenant.acceptance.test.mjs'],working);if(green.exitCode)throw Error('increment1');\\nawait writeFile(working+'/types.ts',`export type TenantId = string;\\\\nexport type Sku = string;\\\\nexport interface Item { sku: Sku; quantity: number }\\\\nexport type TenantBackend = Map<TenantId, Map<Sku, number>>;\\\\nexport interface TenantInventoryPort { readonly tenantId: TenantId; put(sku: Sku, quantity: number): void; get(sku: Sku): number; remove(sku: Sku): void }\\\\n`);\\nawait writeFile(working+'/inventory.d.mts',`import type { TenantBackend, TenantId, Sku, TenantInventoryPort } from './types.js';\\\\nexport declare class TenantInventory implements TenantInventoryPort { readonly tenantId: TenantId; constructor(tenantId: TenantId, sharedBackend?: TenantBackend); put(sku: Sku, quantity: number): void; get(sku: Sku): number; remove(sku: Sku): void; }\\\\n`);\\nawait writeFile(working+'/consumer.mts',`import { TenantInventory } from './inventory.mjs';import type { TenantBackend, TenantInventoryPort } from './types.js';\\\\nconst backend: TenantBackend = new Map();const a: TenantInventoryPort = new TenantInventory('company', backend);a.put('sprocket',7);const n:number=a.get('sprocket');void n;\\\\n// @ts-expect-error owner is immutable\\\\na.tenantId='other';\\\\n// @ts-expect-error ownership is required\\\\nnew TenantInventory();\\\\n// @ts-expect-error quantities are numeric\\\\na.put('sprocket','7');\\\\n`);\\nawait writeFile(working+'/example.mjs',`import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';const shared=new Map();const company=new TenantInventory('company',shared),supplier=new TenantInventory('supplier',shared);company.put('sprocket',7);supplier.put('sprocket',2);console.log(JSON.stringify({company:company.get('sprocket'),supplier:supplier.get('sprocket')}));\\\\n`);\\nawait writeFile(working+'/README.md',`# Tenant-owned inventory\\\\n\\\\nUse TenantInventory(tenantId, sharedBackend?) with a nonblank string owner. Reuse one Map for storage shared by instances; it contains one independent SKU Map per tenant. Separate owners can use the same SKU. Instances with the same owner intentionally share values. Without a supplied backend, an instance has private storage. The domain has no serialized or concatenated composite keys.\\\\n\\\\nPublic API: readonly tenantId, put(sku, quantity), get(sku) and remove(sku). SKU must be a nonblank string; quantity must be a nonnegative integer. Missing SKUs return zero. Invalid input or a corrupt tenant bucket throws. Input validation is runtime behavior; TypeScript declarations describe the API but cannot validate stored values.\\\\n\\\\nMigration: Inventory and InventoryPort are removed in this complete inert package. Replace new Inventory() with new TenantInventory(explicitOwner, sharedBackend). Replace InventoryPort with TenantInventoryPort. Choose the owner at the application boundary before importing old single-owner values into that owner's bucket. There is no implicit default tenant or compatibility wrapper. Existing callers must make that ownership decision; no production data migration is claimed.\\\\n\\\\nRun bun test for runtime behavior and bun example.mjs for two owners sharing one backend. types.ts and inventory.d.mts describe ownership/storage and the consumer.mts fixture checks required owner, read-only owner and numeric quantities with an existing strict compiler. No dependency installation is needed.\\\\n`);\\nawait writeFile(working+'/boundary.test.mjs',`import{test,expect}from'bun:test';import{TenantInventory}from'./inventory.mjs';\\\\ntest('same owner shares one bucket',()=>{let s=new Map(),a=new TenantInventory('a',s),b=new TenantInventory('a',s);a.put('s',4);expect(b.get('s')).toBe(4);b.remove('s');expect(a.get('s')).toBe(0)});\\\\ntest('opaque delimiter identifiers cannot collide',()=>{let s=new Map(),a=new TenantInventory('a:b',s),b=new TenantInventory('a',s);a.put('c',5);b.put('b:c',9);expect(a.get('c')).toBe(5);expect(b.get('b:c')).toBe(9)});\\\\ntest('owner and public input boundary',()=>{for(let id of['', ' ', null, undefined, 4])expect(()=>new TenantInventory(id)).toThrow();let a=new TenantInventory('a');for(let sku of['',' ',null,7])for(let m of['get','remove'])expect(()=>a[m](sku)).toThrow();for(let q of[-1,1.2,Infinity,NaN,'2'])expect(()=>a.put('s',q)).toThrow();expect(()=>{a.tenantId='b'}).toThrow();expect(a.tenantId).toBe('a')});\\\\ntest('backend shape and corruption',()=>{expect(()=>new this.#items().set(sku, quantity); }\\\\n  get(sku) { identifier(sku, 'sku'); return this.#items().get(sku) ?? 0; }\\\\n  remove(sku) { identifier(sku, 'sku'); this.#items().delete(sku); }\\\\n}\\\\n`);\\nawait writeFile(working+'/baseline.test.mjs',`import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';test('migrated single-owner baseline',()=>{const i=new TenantInventory('baseline');i.put('s',3);expect(i.get('s')).toBe(3);i.remove('s');expect(i.get('s')).toBe(0);expect(()=>i.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\\\\n`);\\nlet green=await run('increment1 baseline and unchanged tenant acceptance GREEN',['bun','test','baseline.test.mjs','tenant.acceptance.test.mjs'],working);if(green.exitCode)throw Error('increment1');\\nawait writeFile(working+'/types.ts',`export type TenantId = string;\\\\nexport type Sku = string;\\\\nexport interface Item { sku: Sku; remove(sku: Sku): void }\\\\n`);\\nawait writeFile(working+'/inventory.d.mts',`import type { TenantBackend, TenantId, Sku, TenantInventoryPort } from './types.js';\\\\nexport declare class TenantInventory implements TenantInventoryPort { readonly tenantId: TenantId; Reuse one Map for storage shared by instances; it contains one independent SKU Map per tenant. Separate owners can use the same SKU. Instances with the same owner intentionally share values. Without a supplied backend, an instance has private storage. The domain has no serialized or concatenated composite keys.\\\\n\\\\nPublic API: readonly tenantId, put(sku, quantity), get(sku) and remove(sku). SKU must be a nonblank string; quantity must be a nonnegative integer. Missing SKUs return zero. Invalid input or a corrupt tenant bucket throws. Input validation is runtime behavior; TypeScript declarations describe the API but cannot validate stored values.\\\\n\\\\nMigration: Inventory and InventoryPort are removed in this complete inert package. Replace new Inventory() with new TenantInventory(explicitOwner, sharedBackend). Replace InventoryPort with TenantInventoryPort. Choose the owner at the application boundary before importing old single-owner values into that owner's bucket. There is no implicit default tenant or compatibility wrapper. Existing callers must make that ownership decision; no production data migration is claimed.\\\\n\\\\nRun bun test for runtime behavior and bun example.mjs for two owners sharing one backend. types.ts and inventory.d.mts describe ownership/storage and the consumer.mts fixture checks required owner, read-only owner and numeric quantities with an existing strict compiler. TenantInventory('a',s);a.put('c',5);b.put('b:c',9);expect(a.get('c')).toBe(5);expect(b.get('b:c')).toBe(9)});\\\\ntest('owner and public input boundary',()=>{for(let id of['', ' ', null, undefined, 4])expect(()=>new TenantInventory(id)).toThrow();let a=new TenantInventory('a');for(let sku of['',' ',null,7])for(let m of['get','remove'])expect(()=>a[m](sku)).toThrow();for(let q of[-1,1.2,Infinity,NaN,'2'])expect(()=>a.put('s',q)).toThrow();expect(()=>{a.tenantId='b'}).toThrow();expect(a.tenantId).toBe('a')});\\\\ntest('backend shape and corruption',()=>{expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',{})).toThrow();expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',new Map([['a',1]]))).toThrow();let s=new Map(),a=new       "sha256": "181c57d0992dc16405ed022ad74408d5718cd7030ee3a63d260e858e4bb2d59b",
      "owner": "local-013-direct-06-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import{mkdir,readFile,cp,writeFile}from'node:fs/promises';import{createHash}from'node:crypto';\nconst root=import.meta.dir,out=root+'/outputs',working=out+'/working',records=[];await mkdir(out,{recursive:true});\nconst hash=b=>createHash('sha256').update(b).digest('hex');\nlet reads=[];for(const name of['inventory.mjs','types.ts','README.md','example.mjs','baseline.test.mjs','tenant.acceptance.test.mjs']){let b=await readFile(root+'/input/input/'+name);reads.push({name,sha256:hash(b),bytes:b.length,text:b.toString()})}await writeFile(out+'/affected-source-read.json',JSON.stringify(reads,null,2)+'\\n');\nasync function run(label,argv,cwd){let start=new Date().toISOString(),p=Bun.spawn(argv,{cwd,stdout:'pipe',stderr:'pipe'}),[stdout,stderr,exitCode]=await Promise.all([new Response(p.stdout).text(),new Response(p.stderr).text(),p.exited]);let r={label,argv,cwd,startedAt:start,finishedAt:new Date().toISOString(),exitCode,stdout,stderr};records.push(r);await writeFile(out+'/checks.json',JSON.stringify(records,null,2)+'\\n');return r}\nlet old=await run('original single-tenant baseline GREEN',['bun','test','baseline.test.mjs'],root+'/input/input');if(old.exitCode!==0)throw Error('baseline');let red=await run('original immutable tenant acceptance RED',['bun','test','tenant.acceptance.test.mjs'],root+'/input/input');if(red.exitCode===0)throw Error('acceptance unexpectedly green');\nawait writeFile(out+'/design.md',`# Tenant-first design before implementation\\n\\nThe original Inventory gives each object one private SKU Map and has no ownership boundary. Adding a tenant parameter to put/get would preserve the wrong root and repeat ownership at every call. Concatenating tenant and SKU introduces delimiter collisions.\\n\\nThe domain root is instead TenantInventory with a required, immutable tenantId. Shared storage is Map<tenantId, Map<sku,quantity>>; identity is structurally separated before values are serialized. Each constructor validates owner and backend. Each method validates its public input. Two instances of the same owner share one bucket; different owners cannot observe/remove each other's SKU.\\n\\nIncrement 1 replaces the root and migrates the fixture baseline, then runs baseline+unchanged acceptance. Increment 2 propagates ownership through API declarations, type consumer, docs, runnable example and adjacent checks, then executes all checks. Inventory export is deliberately removed; the migration is explicit rather than retained as a throwaway wrapper. All edits stay in outputs/working and pristine committed inputs remain outside.\\n`);\nawait cp(root+'/input/input',working,{recursive:true});\nawait writeFile(working+'/inventory.mjs',`const identifier = (value, label) => { if (typeof value !== 'string' || value.trim() === '') throw new Error('invalid ' + label); return value; };\\nexport class TenantInventory {\\n  #tenantId; #backend;\\n  constructor(tenantId, sharedBackend = new Map()) {\\n    this.#tenantId = identifier(tenantId, 'tenant');\\n    if (!(sharedBackend instanceof Map)) throw new Error('invalid backend');\\n    const bucket = sharedBackend.get(tenantId);\\n    if (bucket !== undefined && !(bucket instanceof Map)) throw new Error('invalid tenant storage');\\n    if (bucket === undefined) sharedBackend.set(tenantId, new Map());\\n    this.#backend = sharedBackend;\\n    Object.defineProperty(this, 'tenantId', {value: tenantId, writable: false, configurable: false, enumerable: true});\\n  }\\n  #items() { const items = this.#backend.get(this.#tenantId); if (!(items instanceof Map)) throw new Error('invalid tenant storage'); return items; }\\n  put(sku, quantity) { identifier(sku, 'sku'); if (!Number.isInteger(quantity) || quantity < 0) throw new Error('invalid quantity'); this.#items().set(sku, quantity); }\\n  get(sku) { identifier(sku, 'sku'); return this.#items().get(sku) ?? 0; }\\n  remove(sku) { identifier(sku, 'sku'); this.#items().delete(sku); }\\n}\\n`);\nawait writeFile(working+'/baseline.test.mjs',`import {test,expect} from 'bun:test';import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';test('migrated single-owner baseline',()=>{const i=new TenantInventory('baseline');i.put('s',3);expect(i.get('s')).toBe(3);i.remove('s');expect(i.get('s')).toBe(0);expect(()=>i.put('s',-1)).toThrow()});\\n`);\nlet green=await run('increment1 baseline and unchanged tenant acceptance GREEN',['bun','test','baseline.test.mjs','tenant.acceptance.test.mjs'],working);if(green.exitCode)throw Error('increment1');\nawait writeFile(working+'/types.ts',`export type TenantId = string;\\nexport type Sku = string;\\nexport interface Item { sku: Sku; quantity: number }\\nexport type TenantBackend = Map<TenantId, Map<Sku, number>>;\\nexport interface TenantInventoryPort { readonly tenantId: TenantId; put(sku: Sku, quantity: number): void; get(sku: Sku): number; remove(sku: Sku): void }\\n`);\nawait writeFile(working+'/inventory.d.mts',`import type { TenantBackend, TenantId, Sku, TenantInventoryPort } from './types.js';\\nexport declare class TenantInventory implements TenantInventoryPort { readonly tenantId: TenantId; constructor(tenantId: TenantId, sharedBackend?: TenantBackend); put(sku: Sku, quantity: number): void; get(sku: Sku): number; remove(sku: Sku): void; }\\n`);\nawait writeFile(working+'/consumer.mts',`import { TenantInventory } from './inventory.mjs';import type { TenantBackend, TenantInventoryPort } from './types.js';\\nconst backend: TenantBackend = new Map();const a: TenantInventoryPort = new TenantInventory('company', backend);a.put('sprocket',7);const n:number=a.get('sprocket');void n;\\n// @ts-expect-error owner is immutable\\na.tenantId='other';\\n// @ts-expect-error ownership is required\\nnew TenantInventory();\\n// @ts-expect-error quantities are numeric\\na.put('sprocket','7');\\n`);\nawait writeFile(working+'/example.mjs',`import {TenantInventory} from './inventory.mjs';const shared=new Map();const company=new TenantInventory('company',shared),supplier=new TenantInventory('supplier',shared);company.put('sprocket',7);supplier.put('sprocket',2);console.log(JSON.stringify({company:company.get('sprocket'),supplier:supplier.get('sprocket')}));\\n`);\nawait writeFile(working+'/README.md',`# Tenant-owned inventory\\n\\nUse TenantInventory(tenantId, sharedBackend?) with a nonblank string owner. Reuse one Map for storage shared by instances; it contains one independent SKU Map per tenant. Separate owners can use the same SKU. Instances with the same owner intentionally share values. Without a supplied backend, an instance has private storage. The domain has no serialized or concatenated composite keys.\\n\\nPublic API: readonly tenantId, put(sku, quantity), get(sku) and remove(sku). SKU must be a nonblank string; quantity must be a nonnegative integer. Missing SKUs return zero. Invalid input or a corrupt tenant bucket throws. Input validation is runtime behavior; TypeScript declarations describe the API but cannot validate stored values.\\n\\nMigration: Inventory and InventoryPort are removed in this complete inert package. Replace new Inventory() with new TenantInventory(explicitOwner, sharedBackend). Replace InventoryPort with TenantInventoryPort. Choose the owner at the application boundary before importing old single-owner values into that owner's bucket. There is no implicit default tenant or compatibility wrapper. Existing callers must make that ownership decision; no production data migration is claimed.\\n\\nRun bun test for runtime behavior and bun example.mjs for two owners sharing one backend. types.ts and inventory.d.mts describe ownership/storage and the consumer.mts fixture checks required owner, read-only owner and numeric quantities with an existing strict compiler. No dependency installation is needed.\\n`);\nawait writeFile(working+'/boundary.test.mjs',`import{test,expect}from'bun:test';import{TenantInventory}from'./inventory.mjs';\\ntest('same owner shares one bucket',()=>{let s=new Map(),a=new TenantInventory('a',s),b=new TenantInventory('a',s);a.put('s',4);expect(b.get('s')).toBe(4);b.remove('s');expect(a.get('s')).toBe(0)});\\ntest('opaque delimiter identifiers cannot collide',()=>{let s=new Map(),a=new TenantInventory('a:b',s),b=new TenantInventory('a',s);a.put('c',5);b.put('b:c',9);expect(a.get('c')).toBe(5);expect(b.get('b:c')).toBe(9)});\\ntest('owner and public input boundary',()=>{for(let id of['', ' ', null, undefined, 4])expect(()=>new TenantInventory(id)).toThrow();let a=new TenantInventory('a');for(let sku of['',' ',null,7])for(let m of['get','remove'])expect(()=>a[m](sku)).toThrow();for(let q of[-1,1.2,Infinity,NaN,'2'])expect(()=>a.put('s',q)).toThrow();expect(()=>{a.tenantId='b'}).toThrow();expect(a.tenantId).toBe('a')});\\ntest('backend shape and corruption',()=>{expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',{})).toThrow();expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',new Map([['a',1]]))).toThrow();let s=new Map(),a=new TenantInventory('a',s);s.set('a',null);expect(()=>a.get('s')).toThrow()});\\ntest('private storage has no accidental shared tenant',()=>{let a=new TenantInventory('same'),b=new TenantInventory('same');a.put('s',1);expect(b.get('s')).toBe(0)});\\n`);\nlet runtime=await run('increment2 complete runtime suite',['bun','test'],working);if(runtime.exitCode)throw Error('runtime');\nlet compile=await run('strict public API types and negative consumer checks',['bun','/Users/digital-hawk/.bun/install/cache/typescript@5.9.3@@@1/lib/tsc.js','--noEmit','--strict','--target','ES2022','--module','NodeNext','--moduleResolution','NodeNext','consumer.mts','types.ts'],working);if(compile.exitCode)throw Error('compile');\nlet example=await run('actual runnable two-tenant example',['bun','example.mjs'],working);if(example.exitCode||example.stdout.trim()!==JSON.stringify({company:7,supplier:2}))throw Error('example');\nlet acceptance=reads.find(x=>x.name==='tenant.acceptance.test.mjs');if(hash(await readFile(working+'/tenant.acceptance.test.mjs'))!==acceptance.sha256)throw Error('acceptance changed');\nawait run('pristine tracked task input remains unchanged',['git','status','--porcelain'],root+'/input');\nawait writeFile(out+'/summary.json',JSON.stringify({caseId:'new-requirement',attemptId:'direct-06-new-requirement',sourceFreeze:'51f209daf83cd97d64f89881d66533bb59dc57db',inputRevision:'5146b42e798a406216aba005ce1f56470d6ce77c',completedAt:new Date().toISOString(),runtimeChecks:7,staticChecks:'Existing compiler, strict declarations and representative public consumer including expected type errors; not a proof that JS implementation itself was statically checked.',acceptanceUnchanged:true,scope:'Inert local fixture only; no application integration or production migration claimed.',checks:records.map(({label,exitCode})=>({label,exitCode})),gradeAssigned:false},null,2)+'\\n');\nconsole.log(JSON.stringify({checks:records.map(({label,exitCode})=>({label,exitCode})),acceptanceUnchanged:true,gradeAssigned:false},null,2));\n"
    },

      "owner": "local-013-direct-06-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "import{test,expect}from'bun:test';import{TenantInventory}from'./inventory.mjs';\ntest('same owner shares one bucket',()=>{let s=new Map(),a=new TenantInventory('a',s),b=new TenantInventory('a',s);a.put('s',4);expect(b.get('s')).toBe(4);b.remove('s');expect(a.get('s')).toBe(0)});\ntest('opaque delimiter identifiers cannot collide',()=>{let s=new Map(),a=new TenantInventory('a:b',s),b=new TenantInventory('a',s);a.put('c',5);b.put('b:c',9);expect(a.get('c')).toBe(5);expect(b.get('b:c')).toBe(9)});\ntest('owner and public input boundary',()=>{for(let id of['', ' ', null, undefined, 4])expect(()=>new TenantInventory(id)).toThrow();let a=new TenantInventory('a');for(let sku of['',' ',null,7])for(let m of['get','remove'])expect(()=>a[m](sku)).toThrow();for(let q of[-1,1.2,Infinity,NaN,'2'])expect(()=>a.put('s',q)).toThrow();expect(()=>{a.tenantId='b'}).toThrow();expect(a.tenantId).toBe('a')});\ntest('backend shape and corruption',()=>{expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',{})).toThrow();expect(()=>new TenantInventory('a',new Map([['a',1]]))).toThrow();let s=new Map(),a=new TenantInventory('a',s);s.set('a',null);expect(()=>a.get('s')).toThrow()});\ntest('private storage has no accidental shared tenant',()=>{let a=new TenantInventory('same'),b=new TenantInventory('same');a.put('s',1);expect(b.get('s')).toBe(0)});\n"
    },

    {
      "path": "direct-repairs/new-requirement/outputs/design.md",
      "bytes": 1137,
      "sha256": "e252a7ffef2e869eab1a8f7630c620901c43ac7f91a54d21c9366db9ab4791e9",
      "owner": "local-013-direct-06-new-requirement",
      "kind": "case-owned-reviewed-artifact",
      "text": "# Tenant-first design before implementation\n\nThe original Inventory gives each object one private SKU Map and has no ownership boundary. Adding a tenant parameter to put/get would preserve the wrong root and repeat ownership at every call. Concatenating tenant and SKU introduces delimiter collisions.\n\nThe domain root is instead TenantInventory with a required, immutable tenantId. Shared storage is Map<tenantId, Map<sku,quantity>>; identity is structurally separated before values are serialized. Each constructor validates owner and backend. Each method validates its public input. Two instances of the same owner share one bucket; different owners cannot observe/remove each other's SKU.\n\nIncrement 1 replaces the root and migrates the fixture baseline, then runs baseline+unchanged acceptance. Increment 2 propagates ownership through API declarations, type consumer, docs, runnable example and adjacent checks, then executes all checks. Inventory export is deliberately removed; the migration is explicit rather than retained as a throwaway wrapper. All edits stay in outputs/working and pristine committed inputs remain outside.\n"
    },
